RUS_offensive_category = { 
	RUS_vyshgorod_offensive_plan = {
		icon = GFX_RUS_general_staff
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 7
		days_re_enable = 40
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 73
				state = 83
			}
		}
		visible = {
			original_tag = RUS
			has_completed_focus = RUS_resumption_of_active_offensive_actions
		}
		available = {
			NOT = {
					73 = {
						is_fully_controlled_by = controller
						CONTROLLER = {
							OR = {
								original_tag = RUS
								has_war_with = UKR
							}
						}
					}
					83 = {
						is_fully_controlled_by = controller
						CONTROLLER = {
							OR = {
								original_tag = RUS
								has_war_with = UKR
							}
						}
					}
			}
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = RUS_active_offensive_plan_flag
				}
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = RUS_offensive_plan_bonus_variable_tooltip
				check_variable = {
					RUS_offensive_plan_bonus_variable  < 0 
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			UKR = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { controls_state = 73 }
					NOT = { controls_state = 83 }
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = {
				days = 27
				flag = RUS_active_offensive_plan_flag
				value = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				RUS_offensive_plan_bonus_variable = -1
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			73 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = RUS_offensive_plan_bonus_modifier scope = RUS days = 20 } }
			83 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = RUS_offensive_plan_bonus_modifier scope = RUS days = 20 } }
		}
	}
